**The order (six areas):** (1) the Aniyomi uninstall's in-app confirm fires nothing on the device — replace it with the SYSTEM's own uninstall prompt; (2) the Link Source sheet's UI rejected — Aniyomi/CloudStream in two COLUMNS scrolling like an alarm clock's hour/minute drums, per-source ICONS, a proper search bar; (3) the search bar + the search button placement (button to the RIGHT of the bar, not below); (4) the extension settings' Filters/Settings buttons as PILLS with a gap + the filters themselves (a dedicated search view, a capped language menu, ascending/descending toggles); (5) long-press on extension repositories copies the URL; (6) THE BIG ONE — a new Extension Testing screen (ping / search / home page / episode list / details / video resolve / stream play, both ecosystems, batch runs, modular per-test files, built for future automation + statistics). The notification system stays as-is per the user (hard to test without real notifications — progresses alongside).

**The implementation (all on the mainline, 23 files):** D-571 the uninstall flow (the three in-app Aniyomi dialogs REMOVED — the trash icon fires the system uninstaller directly; `uninstallApk` hardened into a logged 3-rung intent ladder with toasts; the REAL silent bug — the CS `uninstallPlugin`'s unguarded loader throw — now caught, surfaced via `uninstallError` StateFlow + toast, `refreshLocked()` in finally). D-572 the filters bar (pills + dedicated search view + capped language menu + asc/desc flip on the active sort + NSFW as its own pill; `sortAscending` threaded through both tabs). D-573 the main search bar's filled right-edge submit button. D-574 repo long-press copy (haptic + clipboard + toast + discoverability hint). D-575 the ManualSearchSheet REWRITE (two snap-fling wheel columns with a center band, falloff and rim fades; per-source icons both ecosystems; the active wheel's centered source is the search target; the pill search bar with the RIGHT circular button; a results view with a change-source chip; `:data:cloudstream` added to anime-details). D-576 THE EXTENSION TESTING SCREEN (the `testing/` package: models / context / engine / chain / SEVEN one-file tests / the screen; `ExtensionTestingKey` + the MainActivity branch + the top-of-screen entry banner; batch selection + a sequential queue + Stop; a user-editable test query; CS resolve routed through the real link resolver, captured field-by-field so `:core:cs-player` stays off the module graph).

**Review (2 rounds):** self-review (the repo's tokenizer balance ×22 files — ALL BALANCED) + an independent review agent that verified every referenced symbol against the source APIs and found THREE compile blockers (the missing `rememberScrollState` import; `Alignment.TopCenter` used via ColumnScope.align in the new screen; the `CsVideoLink` classpath trap) + one behavior defect (cancel marked killed targets finished/healthy) — ALL FIXED, plus three nits applied (dead state, unused imports, the uninstallError overwrite race). The release-screen exclusion from `allowedUpdateSheetKeys` is a documented conscious decision (a batch run must not be interrupted).

**Docs:** `DOCUMENTATION/cloudstream-v2/64-ROUND-82-EXTENSION-TESTING-SCREEN.md` (the testing architecture + the future roadmap: automated runs, statistics, per-extension detail pages — all hanging off the same models). Memory: D-571..D-576 prepended; this record; changelog; SESSION refreshed.

**SHIPPED (v1.1.39 LIVE):** the implementation cycle cost 3 runs — run 35904635970 (01106f07) FAILED fast (three error classes: the BasicTextField import package, a content-less Surface call in the wheel band, the CsVideoLink compile-classpath rule) and run 35905119703 (b89d2413) FAILED fast (one stale duplicate import survived the cleanup) — each fixed from the logs, no blind retries; run 35905541634 (6ad76dee) GREEN. The D-498 cut: release/1.1.39 from the green head → the 1.1.39/10139 bump (the D-571..D-576 story in the block comment) → annotated tag v1.1.39 → **Release APK run 35906141111 GREEN first-try** → verified via the API: stable, --latest, ani-kuta-v1.1.39-debug-arm64-v8a.apk 68.0 MB + SHA256SUMS.txt, body = the tag bullets. 10139 > 10138 → in-app update. **CI ledger DISCLOSED: 4 runs total this cycle (2 fast-fail compile runs + 1 green implementation + 1 green release) — over the ≤2 budget, the cost of a brand-new screen with CI as the only compiler; every failure diagnosed from logs before the next push.** The user's device round: (1) Aniyomi extension uninstall — trash icon → the SYSTEM prompt (app name + "Do you want to uninstall this app?" + Cancel/OK) → OK removes it; (2) the Link Source wheels — snap feel, icons, tap-to-center, search bar; (3) the search screens' right-edge button; (4) the filters pills + dedicated search + language cap + sort flip; (5) repo long-press copy; (6) the Extension Testing screen — run all, per-test rows, batch, CS resolve path.

## Round 83 (2026-09-24) — the v1.1.39 device round: the uninstall permission, the 19ms testing bug fixed + the suite reworked, the icon-pill header, the polished filters, and the linked-source-aware sheet (D-577..D-579)

**The order (the v1.1.39 device report, five areas):** (1) the uninstall fix STILL does nothing on the device — no prompt, nothing on screen (log provided: UninstallerActivity starts then FINISHES ~70ms in); (2) the Link Source sheet: separate dedicated section cards with distinct backgrounds for Aniyomi/CloudStream, PRE-SELECT + highlight the currently LINKED source (the sheet highlighted the wrong entries), the "The centered source is the one that gets searched." hint tail removed, a stronger center-only highlight, a compact sheet, and the search bar reworked per an exact spec (X at the very right edge, the leading magnifier hiding on text/focus, the circular button only while the bar is in use, a border, bottom padding); (3) the extensions filters: polish the Language + Sort menus and the dedicated search field; (4) repo copy verified WORKING (untouched); (5) the Extension Testing screen: the entry becomes an ICON pill alongside Filters/Settings (all icon-only), the UI reworked, each system testable SEPARATELY, tap/long-press multi-select (tap-to-expand removed), the anime extensions' instant-fail bug fixed (search failed in 19ms on a KNOWN-WORKING extension), smart well-known search phrases across anime/series/movie categories, a beautiful live batch UI with a proper summary, human time (seconds/minutes, not raw ms), and PERSISTENT results memory with re-test-failed / re-test-passed actions.

**The implementation (18 Kotlin files + the manifest, 3 new D decisions):** D-577 the uninstall root cause — the manifest carried REQUEST_INSTALL_PACKAGES only; the platform UninstallerActivity REQUIRES `REQUEST_DELETE_PACKAGES` on the CALLING app or it starts-and-finishes silently (~70ms, exactly the reported log); one permission line + comment. D-578 the 19ms bug — the round-82 test files called the source methods on the MAIN dispatcher (real aniyomi extensions do network at Rx-subscription time on the calling thread → instant NetworkOnMainThreadException; the CS bridge dispatches internally, which is why CS passed); all five test files now wrap in `withContext(Dispatchers.IO)` (matching SearchViewModel's production pattern). The SMART SEARCH ladder (`TestingSearchPhrases.kt`): the user's custom query first, then One Piece / Naruto / Breaking Bad / Interstellar (anime/series/movie categories), first attempt with results wins, per-attempt 12s inner bound, the kind timeout raised to 60s, the aggregate failure reports what happened per attempt. `TestTimeFormat.kt` — human durations everywhere ("1.2 s", "2m 05s"). The testing screen rework: icon-pill header (Science · Filter · Gear, banner gone), All/Aniyomi/CloudStream chips + per-section select-all/run, tap AND long-press toggle selection (haptic; chevron is the only expand), the `TestingBatchOverlay` (Testing 3 of 7 + animated progress + the live per-test rows + Stop), the `TestingSummaryCard` (animated pass/fail/untested proportion bar + "Failed (n)"/"Passed (n)" precision re-runs + Clear results), and the modular file split (TestingTargetCard / TestingBatchOverlay / TestingSummaryCard / TestingSearchPhrases / TestTimeFormat / ExtensionTestResultStore). D-579 the MEMORY — `ExtensionTestResultStore`: per-target JSON documents in SharedPreferences; saved the moment each target finishes (a crash mid-batch never loses completed verdicts); restored on screen open (last verdict chips visible immediately); uninstalled targets pruned; Clear wipes. The sheet rework: linked-source pre-selection (id match, name fallback) + a persistent ✓ on the linked row + the active side defaulting to the linked ecosystem; dedicated section cards (surfaceVariant vs secondaryContainer) with card-matched rim fades; center-only full highlight; the short hint; the wrap-content sheet; the exact-spec search bar. The filters: styled Language/Sort menus (surfaceContainerHigh, 16dp rounded, header rows, active-tinted+checked/arrowed rows) and the bordered dedicated search field.

**Review:** the nesting-aware tokenizer ×18 touched files ALL BALANCED; the manifest minidom-validated (11 permissions, REQUEST_DELETE_PACKAGES present); full self re-reads (one nullable non-smart-cast fixed pre-push: the batch overlay's currentState). The implementation run FAILED fast (5 errors, two root causes: the four rewritten test files dropped the `.tests` package suffix → the chain's imports unresolved; `Modifier.weight` outside a RowScope in the summary's Segment) — both fixed from the logs, no blind retries.

**Docs:** `DOCUMENTATION/cloudstream-v2/65-ROUND-83-TESTING-SUITE-REWORK.md` (the full anatomy of the permission fix + the 19ms bug, the rework maps, the store design, the v1.1.40 device-round checklist). Memory: D-577..D-579 prepended; this record; changelog; SESSION refreshed; two lessons filed.

## Round 84 (2026-09-24) — the v1.1.40 device round: the five-page testing system + the hard-isolation hang fix, the delete exit choreography, the true pills, the button-feel menus, and the sheet's third pass (D-580..D-583)

**The feedback round:** the user device-tested v1.1.40 end-to-end. The uninstall fix WORKS ("it properly showed me the pop-up… deleted successfully"). The demands: the delete ANIMATION (Downloads parity, both ecosystems), pill-shaped header buttons with proper widths, separated button-feel language/sort options + a cleaner filter search field, the link-source sheet (taller, the right item highlighted, 3D backgrounds, smoother scroll + haptics, a better search bar that opens EMPTY with tap-to-paste), and the testing system — "not satisfactory at all", cluttered, with a dead Stop button and stuck runs — to be redone as a multi-page modular system with a system selector, skip for stuck tests, detailed live per-test results, and a dedicated stats page.

**The hang, root-caused (D-583):** every safety layer was cooperative. Extensions with fully-blocking `suspend fun` overrides (no suspension points) defeat `withTimeout` (it fires only at suspension), `Job.cancel()` (a flag nobody checks), and the batch loop had no recovery — the old screen's `isBatchRunning` then gated every control including Stop. THE FIX: `TestIsolation.runKind` — each kind's body on a DEDICATED single-thread daemon dispatcher, the caller awaiting `deferred.await()` in 250ms slices under its OWN deadline (await is a cancellable suspension — the caller ALWAYS returns at deadline/skip/stop), abort = cancel + `shutdownNow()` interrupt; the engine publishes the kind's dispatcher into `context.ioDispatcher` and all 7 test files route blocking work through it (the interrupt lands on the actual blocked call); SearchTest rethrows CancellationException + disambiguates the attempt-TCE by coroutine liveness; SEARCH 60s→80s (60s was exactly 5×12s); the test client gained `callTimeout(30s)`. A timeout is now a VERDICT ("Timed out after 12 s"), never a hang.

**The five pages (D-583):** 1 HOME — hero proportion bar, the live-run banner (View/Stop), the two ecosystem cards, Run all, Statistics. 2 LIST (`ExtensionTestingListKey`) — NO checkbox column: [icon][name+lang][ONE status chip][chevron]; tap=expand (emphasized animateContentSize), long-press=select (haptic; check badge on the icon); search field, select-all, pinned selection bar. 3 RUN (`ExtensionTestingRunKey(csv)`) — the dedicated full-screen run page (the overlay is dead): animated progress, the current target's live per-kind rows, finished verdicts (tap → detail), the upcoming queue, STOP + SKIP-TARGET always live (the stuck-test escape hatch), completion re-runs. 4 TARGET DETAIL — the seven full verdict cards + per-target re-run. 5 STATS — totals, per-system pass bars, per-kind avg-duration/failure-rate table, the needs-attention list, the run-history feed, Clear. The `ExtensionTestRunController` (app-scoped singleton, GlobalContext DI — zero wiring) owns the lifecycle: the session survives navigation, Stop/Skip are always wired, verdicts persist per-target the moment each finishes, and every run appends a `StoredRunSummary` (capped 40) to the store's `history` key. 4 new `@Serializable` keys + the `pushTesting` duplicate-class guard (the Task-62 crash). `abortedByUser` lands in models + store (migration-free) — a skipped target never counts healthy. Deleted: ExtensionTestingScreen/TestingTargetCard/TestingBatchOverlay/TestingSummaryCard; shared visuals consolidated in `TestingSharedUi.kt`.

**The UI rounds (D-580/D-581/D-582):** the shared DeleteExitState choreography in ExtensionListChrome (110ms settle dip → 240ms slide+fade via draw-phase graphicsLayer; delete fires after the exit) — Aniyomi rows optimistically (a 3s watcher fades the row back if the system uninstall was dismissed), CS rows after their AlertDialog confirm; `animateItem()` on every row of both tabs. Tests/Filters/Settings became TRUE pills (icon + label, adaptive widths). The language/sort menus became bordered button-feel option cards with 4dp separation; the filter search field gained a steady magnifier + a focus ring + a boxed clear. The sheet: wheel 186→236dp + a 60%-of-screen minimum; the wrong-initial-highlight BUG fixed (center emissions only move the selection — activeSide changes only on user-driven taps/drag-settles); 3D cards (animated shadow, primary rim, gradient sheen); the falloff moved into graphicsLayer + lightTick haptics; the search bar opens EMPTY ("Search <source>…") and the first focus pastes the content name.

**Review + CI:** nesting-aware tokenizer + package-line sweep on every touched file (the round-83 lessons applied); the engine/controller seam re-read in full. CI ledger: run 1 (UI batch, 839c7c8) GREEN first-try; run 2 (testing system, 713ba6e) FAILED on 4 errors — a dropped `?.` in a chain, a smart-cast not extending into an items lambda, the parent-package `matchesSearch` import, an implicit-receiver AnimatedVisibility capturing the deprecated RowScope overload — all diagnosed from the logs, fixed in one push; run 3 GREEN (23eab3f). The D-498 cut follows this record (v1.1.41).

**Docs:** `DOCUMENTATION/cloudstream-v2/66-ROUND-84-TESTING-FIVE-PAGE-SYSTEM.md`; D-580..D-583 prepended; this record; changelog; 4 lessons; SESSION refreshed.


## Round 85 (2026-09-24) — the v1.1.41 device round: honest stop/settle semantics, in-place runs, rich payloads, the stats dashboard, the hub rework, the sheet's fourth pass, and the confirmed-removal choreography (D-584..D-586)

**The feedback round:** the user device-tested v1.1.41 across the Link Source sheet, the extensions screen, and all five testing pages — the most detailed report the testing system has received: the stop/rerun logic glitches ("marked stopped but still shows now testing", "the test did not rerun"), runs forcing navigation to a new screen ("it should run the tests on that same screen"), the list expansion leaking per-test detail ("only the simple tests and their time duration should be shown" + actions belong at the top), the trailing arrow, the selection-mode tap conflict, video resolve giving up "after about five seconds", One Piece as a 1100-episode chain target + the missing Chinese anime phrase, the demand to show the ACTUAL results (search results, home entries, details, episodes, videos, stream bytes), the single-bar stats page ("no circular donuts, graphs, bars"), the "off" hub buttons, the sheet's dead space + keyboard-over-search-bar + no-blur-reset + unsorted wheels, the pills' text ("there should only be the icons"), and the delete animation firing before the confirm ("when the user actually clicks OK… THEN the animation should play" — and the same for trust/untrust). The user also ordered a UI-design-agent prompt FIRST (delivered to the upload folder: ANI-KUTA-UI-DESIGN-AGENT-PROMPT.md) and five independent research sub-agents on the testing screens (delivered: file:line root-causes for every complaint — their findings drove the fixes).

**D-584 (the run-state machine):** the stop settle now clears currentTargetId + rewrites dangling RUNNING results to SKIPPED("Stopped by user") + marks the interrupted target finished+aborted; the queue starts isRunning=false (a Queued chip for waiting rows); the rerun blocker was the Run page's rememberSaveable armed-set being restored by the nav shell's per-CLASS SaveableStateHolder — now instance-local + armed only after a successful start; IN-PLACE RUNS everywhere (list/per-row/selected/detail/hub call the controller directly — the Run page is a deep view); the list expansion = actions first + compact duration rows only, tap-toggles-selection while selecting, chevron gone, animateItem, the double height-animator resolved; VIDEO RESOLVE patience — the CS resolver no longer coerces a missing/degenerate loadLinksTimeoutMs into a hard 5s wall (the 120s default applies; the 30s first-link watchdog still bounds UX) and the Aniyomi test mirrors the PRODUCTION hoster ladder (getHosterList → lazy getVideoList(hoster) → legacy, ×3 episodes, 90s budget); phrases Jujutsu Kaisen + Link Click (donghua) replace One Piece/Naruto; the DETAILS/EPISODE_LIST stages walk the full search+home candidate pools forward on dead entries.

**D-585 (the rich results + the dashboards):** a TestPayload chain (TestOutcome → TestResult → a JSON store projection → the UI) carries the ACTUAL data: poster cards for search/home, the details dossier, EP chips, server/quality rows (the ABI quality scale for CS), HTTP/RTT/bytes chips for ping/stream — rendered below the run page's tap-expanded rows and inline on the detail page. TestingCharts.kt (animated-sweep donut, grow-in bars, sparkline, count-ups) powers the stats dashboard (health-ring hero + trend + per-system mini donuts + stage-reliability bars + history with wall time) and the hub's new layout (health-ring hero + recent-run chips + the morphing command footer with a badged Stats pill — the stock buttons gone).

**D-586 (the choreography + the sheet + the pills):** the Aniyomi trash fires the system uninstaller DIRECTLY (the row stays visible under the prompt; cancel costs nothing); a screen-local PACKAGE_REMOVED receiver freezes the removed extension as a GHOST at its captured index and the exit choreography plays ONCE on the confirmed removal (animateItem closes the gap); trust/untrust animates the same way on BOTH ecosystems; the sheet loses its 60% dead slab, pads by navigationBars ∪ ime (keyboard never covers the bar; adjustResize on the activity), sorts both wheels alphabetically (remember-keyed), and gains the blur contract (focus loss or keyboard-back resets the field unless results are showing; rememberSaveable state); the header pills are icon-only stadiums with hoisted semantics.

**Review + CI:** the nesting-aware tokenizer across every touched file ALL BALANCED; the manifest minidom-validated; self re-reads caught four nullable/lambda issues pre-push. CI ledger: batch 1 (77f8185) GREEN first-try; batch 2 (6360bae) FAILED on ONE error (the missing TestPayload import in PingTest) — diagnosed from the logs and fixed in the batch-3 push; batch 3 (541a3ec) GREEN. The D-498 cut follows this record (v1.1.42).
